                  [32m"  const doneCards = cards.filter(c => c.column_id === 'done').map(c => ({ id: c.id, title: c.title, position: c.position }))\n"[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    columns: [\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      { id: 'todo', title: 'Todo', cards: todoCards },\n"[39m +
                  [32m"      { id: 'in-progress', title: 'In Progress', cards: inProgressCards },\n"[39m +
                  [32m"      { id: 'done', title: 'Done', cards: doneCards }\n"[39m +
                  [32m'    ]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export const moveCardTransaction = db.transaction((cardId: number, targetCol: string, targetPos: number) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Get the card to move\n'[39m +
                  [32m"  const card = db.prepare('SELECT id, column_id, position FROM cards WHERE id = ?').get(cardId) as Card | undefined\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!card) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    throw new Error(`Card with ID ${cardId} not found`)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const sourceCol = card.column_id\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const sourcePos = card.position\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  // Get total count in target column to clamp targetPos safely\n'[39m +
                  [32m"  const countResult = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM cards WHERE column_id = ?').get(targetCol) as { count: number }\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  const maxPos = sourceCol === targetCol ? countResult.count - 1 : countResult.count\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const clampedTargetPos = Math.max(0, Math.min(targetPos, maxPos))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (sourceCol === targetCol) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Reordering within the same column\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (sourcePos === clampedTargetPos) return\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (sourcePos < clampedTargetPos) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // Moving down\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // Decrement positions of cards in (sourcePos, clampedTargetPos]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        SET position = position - 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        WHERE column_id = ? AND position > ? AND position <= ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      `).run(sourceCol, sourcePos, clampedTargetPos)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // Moving up\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // Increment positions of cards in [clampedTargetPos, sourcePos)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        SET position = position + 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        WHERE column_id = ? AND position >= ? AND position < ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      `).run(sourceCol, clampedTargetPos, sourcePos)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Update the card being moved\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    db.prepare('UPDATE cards SET position = ? WHERE id = ?').run(clampedTargetPos, cardId)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Moving between different columns\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // 1. Decrement positions of cards in source column that were after the moved card\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'      SET position = position - 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'      WHERE column_id = ? AND position > ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    `).run(sourceCol, sourcePos)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // 2. Increment positions of cards in target column that are at or after the target position\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'      SET position = position + 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'      WHERE column_id = ? AND position >= ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    `).run(targetCol, clampedTargetPos)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m"    // 3. Update the moved card's column and position\n"[39m +
                  [32m"    db.prepare('UPDATE cards SET column_id = ?, position = ? WHERE id = ?').run(targetCol, clampedTargetPos, cardId)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'})\n'[39m +
                  [32m''[39m
              },
              providerOptions: {
                vertex: {
                  thoughtSignature:
